Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The riemannian operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 230. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 628 with 5 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the riemannian decomposition. By minimality, the element a^230 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 628-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
